w020717.2 | bootcamp | n. | 集训班, 速成训练营 | a short, intensive course of training | I joined a coding bootcamp last summer. | 去年夏天我参加了一个编程速成训练营。
w020726.1 | carer | n. | 护理者, 照料者 | a person who looks after a sick, old or disabled person | She is a full-time carer for her mother. | 她全职照顾她的母亲。
w020727.1 | carriageway | n. | 车行道, 车道 | the part of a road on which vehicles travel | A lorry broke down on the northbound carriageway. | 一辆卡车在北行车道上抛锚了。
